SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   150	
   151	@Composable
   152	fun CinerificApp() {
   153	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   154	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   155	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   156	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   157	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   158	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   159	    }
   160	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   161	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   162	    var signOutLoadingVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   163	
   164	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   165	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   166	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   167	        signedInProfile = profile
   168	        signInSessionId += 1
   169	        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   170	        showHome = true
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    BackHandler(enabled = !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()) {
   174	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   178	        AndroidView(
   179	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   180	            factory = { context ->
   181	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   182	                    introView = this
   183	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   184	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   185	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   186	                        }
   187	                    }
   188	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   189	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   190	                }
   191	            },
   192	            update = { view ->
   193	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   194	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   195	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   196	                    }
   197	                }
   198	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   199	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   200	            }
   201	        )
   202	
   203	        if (showHome) {
   204	            CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   205	                CinerificMainExperience(
   206	                    signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   207	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   208	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   209	                    onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   210	                    onSignOutLoadingStarted = { signOutLoadingVisible = true },
   211	                    onSignOut = {
   212	                        signOutLoadingVisible = true
   213	                        welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   214	                        signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   215	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   216	                        showHome = false
   217	                        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   218	                    }
   219	                )
   220	            }
   221	        }
   222	
   223	        if (signOutLoadingVisible) {
   224	            SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   225	        }
   226	    }
   227	}
   228	
   229	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   230	    Home,
   231	    Movies,
   232	    Shows,
   233	    Favorites,
   234	    Settings,
   235	    ProgramDetails
   236	}
   237	
   238	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   239	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   240	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   241	) {
   242	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   243	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   244	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   245	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   246	}
   247	
   248	private data class CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   249	    val manufacturer: String,
   250	    val modelName: String,
   251	    val hardwareModel: String,
   252	    val releaseYear: Int?
   253	) {
   254	    val stableId: String
   255	        get() = Base64.encodeToString(
   256	            "$manufacturer|$hardwareModel".lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8),
   257	            Base64.URL_SAFE or Base64.NO_WRAP or Base64.NO_PADDING
   258	        )
   259	
   260	    val displayName: String
   261	        get() = buildString {
   262	            append(manufacturer)
   263	            append(' ')
   264	            append(modelName)
   265	            releaseYear?.let { year ->
   266	                append(" (")
   267	                append(year)
   268	                append(')')
   269	            }
   270	        }
   271	}
   272	
   273	private fun currentCinerificDevice(): CinerificDeviceIdentity {
   274	    val rawManufacturer = Build.MANUFACTURER.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown" }
   275	    val manufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { character ->
   276	        character.titlecase()
   277	    }
   278	    val hardwareModel = Build.MODEL.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown model" }
   279	    val normalizedManufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase()
   280	    val normalizedModel = hardwareModel.uppercase()
   281	    val knownModel = when {
   282	        normalizedManufacturer == "samsung" && normalizedModel.startsWith("SM-G990") ->
   283	            "Galaxy S21 FE 5G" to 2022
   284	        else -> hardwareModel to null
   285	    }
   286	    return CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   287	        manufacturer = manufacturer,
   288	        modelName = knownModel.first,
   289	        hardwareModel = hardwareModel,
   290	        releaseYear = knownModel.second
   291	    )
   292	}
   293	
   294	private fun accountDevicePrefix(profile: CinerificProfile): String =
   295	    "account:${profile.name}"
   296	
   297	private fun recordAccountDeviceUse(
   298	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   299	    profile: CinerificProfile,
   300	    device: CinerificDeviceIdentity
   301	) {
   302	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   303	    val idsKey = accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX
   304	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:${device.stableId}"
   305	    val deviceIds = preferences.getStringSet(idsKey, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
   306	    deviceIds += device.stableId
   307	    preferences.edit()
   308	        .putStringSet(idsKey, deviceIds)
   309	        .putString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", device.manufacturer)
   310	        .putString("$devicePrefix:model", device.modelName)
   311	        .putInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", device.releaseYear ?: 0)
   312	        .putInt(
   313	            "$devicePrefix:use_count",
   314	            preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:use_count", 0) + 1
   315	        )
   316	        .putLong("$devicePrefix:last_used", System.currentTimeMillis())
   317	        .apply()
   318	}
   319	
   320	private fun mostUsedDeviceName(
   321	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   322	    profile: CinerificProfile
   323	): String? {
   324	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   325	    val deviceIds = preferences
   326	        .getStringSet(accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX, emptySet())
   327	        .orEmpty()
   328	    val mostUsedDeviceId = deviceIds.maxWithOrNull(
   329	        compareBy<String> { deviceId ->
   330	            preferences.getInt("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:use_count", 0)
   331	        }.thenBy { deviceId ->
   332	            preferences.getLong("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:last_used", 0L)
   333	        }
   334	    ) ?: return null
   335	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:$mostUsedDeviceId"
   336	    val manufacturer = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", null) ?: return null
   337	    val modelName = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:model", null) ?: return null
   338	    val releaseYear = preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", 0)
   339	    return buildString {
   340	        append(manufacturer)
   341	        append(' ')
   342	        append(modelName)
   343	        if (releaseYear > 0) {
   344	            append(" (")
   345	            append(releaseYear)
   346	            append(')')
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	}
   350	
   351	@Composable
   352	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   353	    signInSessionId: Int,
   354	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   355	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   356	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   357	    onSignOutLoadingStarted: () -> Unit,
   358	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   359	) {
   360	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   361	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   362	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   363	    }
   364	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   365	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   366	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   367	    val context = LocalContext.current
   368	    val playbackStatsPreferences = remember(context) {
   369	        context.getSharedPreferences(PLAYBACK_STATS_PREFERENCES, 0)
   370	    }
   371	    val accountDevicePreferences = remember(context) {
   372	        context.getSharedPreferences(ACCOUNT_DEVICE_PREFERENCES, 0)
   373	    }
   374	    var mostUsedDeviceName by remember(signedInProfile) {
   375	        mutableStateOf(mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile))
   376	    }
   377	    var programPlayCounts by remember {
   378	        mutableStateOf(
   379	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   380	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   381	                val count = value as? Int ?: return@mapNotNull null
   382	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX) to count
   383	            }.toMap()
   384	        )
   385	    }
   386	    var programPlaytimeMillis by remember {
   387	        mutableStateOf(
   388	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   389	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   390	                val durationMillis = value as? Long ?: return@mapNotNull null
   391	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX) to durationMillis
   392	            }.toMap()
   393	        )
   394	    }
   395	    var mostRecentlyPlayedTitle by remember {
   396	        mutableStateOf(
   397	            playbackStatsPreferences.getString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, null)
   398	        )
   399	    }
   400	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   401	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   402	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   403	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   404	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   405	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   406	    var activePlaybackTitle by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
   407	    var activePlaybackLastTickMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   408	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   409	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   410	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   411	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   412	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   413	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   414	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   415	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   416	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   417	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   418	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   419	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   420	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   421	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   422	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   423	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   424	    )
   425	
   426	    LaunchedEffect(signInSessionId, signedInProfile) {
   427	        recordAccountDeviceUse(
   428	            preferences = accountDevicePreferences,
   429	            profile = signedInProfile,
   430	            device = currentCinerificDevice()
   431	        )
   432	        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile)
   433	    }
   434	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   435	        isPortrait,
   436	        destination,
   437	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   438	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   439	    ) {
   440	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   441	            override fun onPostScroll(
   442	                consumed: Offset,
   443	                available: Offset,
   444	                source: NestedScrollSource
   445	            ): Offset {
   446	                if (
   447	                    isPortrait &&
   448	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   449	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   450	                    consumed.y != 0f
   451	                ) {
   452	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   453	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   454	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   455	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   456	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   457	                }
   458	                return Offset.Zero
   459	            }
   460	        }
   461	    }
   462	
   463	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   464	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   465	            isPortrait &&
   466	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   467	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   468	        ) {
   469	            1f
   470	        } else {
   471	            0f
   472	        }
   473	    }
   474	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   475	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   476	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   477	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   478	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   479	            },
   480	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   481	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   482	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   483	            }
   484	        )
   485	    }
   486	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   487	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   488	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   489	        catalogRouteModeName
   490	    ) {
   491	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   492	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   493	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   494	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   495	        )
   496	    }
   497	    val mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = remember(
   498	        programPlayCounts,
   499	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   500	    ) {
   501	        val highestPlayCount = programPlayCounts.values.maxOrNull()
   502	        if (highestPlayCount == null) {
   503	            null
   504	        } else {
   505	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   506	                programPlayCounts[title] == highestPlayCount
   507	            } ?: programPlayCounts.entries.firstOrNull { (_, count) ->
   508	                count == highestPlayCount
   509	            }?.key
   510	        }
   511	    }
   512	    val mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   513	        programPlayCounts[title]
   514	    }
   515	    val longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = remember(
   516	        programPlaytimeMillis,
   517	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   518	    ) {
   519	        val longestDuration = programPlaytimeMillis.values.maxOrNull()
   520	        if (longestDuration == null) {
   521	            null
   522	        } else {
   523	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   524	                programPlaytimeMillis[title] == longestDuration
   525	            } ?: programPlaytimeMillis.entries.firstOrNull { (_, duration) ->
   526	                duration == longestDuration
   527	            }?.key
   528	        }
   529	    }
   530	    val longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   531	        programPlaytimeMillis[title]
   532	    }
   533	
   534	    LaunchedEffect(activePlaybackTitle) {
   535	        val title = activePlaybackTitle ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   536	        while (activePlaybackTitle == title) {
   537	            delay(1_000L)
   538	            if (activePlaybackTitle != title) break
   539	            val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   540	            val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   541	            activePlaybackLastTickMillis = now
   542	            val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   543	            programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   544	            playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   545	                .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   546	                .apply()
   547	        }
   548	    }
   549	
   550	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   551	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   552	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   553	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   554	    }
   555	
   556	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   557	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   558	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   559	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   560	        if (
   561	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   562	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   563	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   564	        ) {
   565	            onSignOut()
   566	        }
   567	    }
   568	
   569	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   570	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   571	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   572	        }
   573	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   574	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   575	    }
   576	
   577	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   578	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   579	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   580	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   581	        destination = route.destination
   582	    }
   583	
   584	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   585	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   586	    }
   587	
   588	    fun recordProgramPlay(title: String) {
   589	        val updatedCount = (programPlayCounts[title] ?: 0) + 1
   590	        programPlayCounts = programPlayCounts + (title to updatedCount)
   591	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle = title
   592	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   593	            .putInt(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedCount)
   594	            .putString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, title)
   595	            .apply()
   596	    }
   597	
   598	    fun startProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   599	        activePlaybackTitle = title
   600	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   601	    }
   602	
   603	    fun stopProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   604	        if (activePlaybackTitle != title) return
   605	        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   606	        val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   607	        val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   608	        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   609	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   610	            .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   611	            .apply()
   612	        activePlaybackTitle = null
   613	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = 0L
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   617	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   618	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   619	            return
   620	        }
   621	
   622	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   623	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   624	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   625	            isShow = isShow
   626	        )
   627	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   628	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   629	            return
   630	        }
   631	
   632	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   633	    }
   634	
   635	    BackHandler {
   636	        when {
   637	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   638	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   639	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   640	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   641	            }
   642	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   643	                clearCatalogRoute()
   644	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   645	            }
   646	            else -> showSignOutConfirmation = true
   647	        }
   648	    }
   649	
   650	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   651	        Box(
   652	            modifier = Modifier
   653	                .fillMaxSize()
   654	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   655	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   656	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   657	                        while (true) {
   658	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   659	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   660	                        }
   661	                    }
   662	                }
   663	        ) {
   664	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   665	                when (destination) {
   666	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   667	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   668	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   669	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   670	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   671	                    )
   672	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   673	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   674	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   675	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   676	                        destination = destination,
   677	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   678	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   679	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   680	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   681	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   682	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   683	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   684	                        },
   685	                        onSignOut = onSignOut,
   686	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   687	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   688	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   689	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   690	                        mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle,
   691	                        mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount,
   692	                        longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle,
   693	                        longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeMillis,
   694	                        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis,
   695	                        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName,
   696	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   697	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   698	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   699	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   700	                    )
   701	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   702	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   703	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   704	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   705	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   706	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   707	                        onProgramPlayed = ::recordProgramPlay,
   708	                        onProgramPlaybackStarted = ::startProgramPlaybackTimer,
   709	                        onProgramPlaybackStopped = ::stopProgramPlaybackTimer,
   710	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   711	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   712	                    )
   713	                }
   714	            }
   715	
   716	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   717	                currentDestination = destination,
   718	                portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   719	                onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   720	                    portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   721	                },
   722	                onDestinationSelected = {
   723	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   724	                    destination = it
   725	                },
   726	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   727	            )
   728	
   729	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   730	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   731	                currentDestination = destination,
   732	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   733	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   734	            )
   735	
   736	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   737	                SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   738	                    onConfirm = {
   739	                        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   740	                        signOutInProgress = true
   741	                        onSignOutLoadingStarted()
   742	                        scope.launch {
   743	                            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   744	                            onSignOut()
   745	                        }
   746	                    },
   747	                    onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   748	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   749	                )
   750	            }
   751	        }
   752	    }
   753	}

   774	@Composable
   775	private fun SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   776	    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
   777	    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
   778	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   779	) {
   780	    val frameShape = RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)
   781	    val panelShape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
   782	    val frameStrokeWidth = 2.dp
   783	    val frameClearSpace = frameStrokeWidth * (10f / 3f)
   784	    val selectionStrokeAlpha = rememberCinerificSelectionStrokeAlpha()
   785	    val selectionStrokeColor = lerpColor(
   786	        Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   787	        Color(0xFFE7E7E7),
   788	        ((selectionStrokeAlpha - 0.5f) / 0.17f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   789	    )
   790	
   791	    Box(
   792	        modifier = modifier,
   793	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   794	    ) {
   795	        Box(
   796	            modifier = Modifier
   797	                .fillMaxSize()
   798	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
   799	                .clickable(onClick = {})
   800	        )
   801	        Box(
   802	            modifier = Modifier
   803	                .fillMaxWidth(0.84f)
   804	                .widthIn(max = 420.dp)
   805	                .border(
   806	                    frameStrokeWidth,
   807	                    selectionStrokeColor,
   808	                    frameShape
   809	                )
   810	                .padding(frameClearSpace)
   811	        ) {
   812	            Column(
   813	                modifier = Modifier
   814	                    .fillMaxWidth()
   815	                    .clip(panelShape)
   816	                    .background(Color(0xFF252126))
   817	                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 22.dp),
   818	                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
   819	                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
   820	            ) {
   821	                Text(
   822	                    text = "Sign out from\nCinerific?",
   823	                    color = Color.White,
   824	                    fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   825	                    fontSize = 26.sp,
   826	                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
   827	                    lineHeight = 34.sp,
   828	                    letterSpacing = 0.55.sp,
   829	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   830	                )
   831	                Row(
   832	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
   833	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   834	                ) {
   835	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   836	                        label = "Yes",
   837	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   838	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   839	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   840	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   841	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   842	                    )
   843	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   844	                        label = "No",
   845	                        backgroundColor = Color(0xFF3A343C),
   846	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   847	                        borderColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.72f),
   848	                        onClick = onDismiss,
   849	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   850	                    )
   851	                }
   852	            }
   853	        }
   854	    }
   855	}

   857	@Composable
   858	private fun SignOutConfirmationButton(
   859	    label: String,
   860	    backgroundColor: Color,
   861	    contentColor: Color,
   862	    borderColor: Color,
   863	    onClick: () -> Unit,
   864	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier

   873	            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
   874	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   875	    ) {

   877	            text = label,
   878	            color = contentColor,
   879	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,

   886	
   887	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   888	    destinationName: String,
   889	    genreName: String,
   890	    modeName: String
   891	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   892	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   893	
   894	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   895	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   896	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   897	
   898	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   899	        destination = destination,
   900	        genre = genre,
   901	        mode = mode
   902	    )
   903	}
   904	
   905	@Composable
   906	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   907	    requestId: Int,
   908	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   909	    suppressed: Boolean,
   910	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   911	) {
   912	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   913	
   914	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   915	        if (suppressed) {
   916	            visible = false
   917	            return@LaunchedEffect
   918	        }
   919	        if (requestId == 0) {
   920	            visible = false
   921	            return@LaunchedEffect
   922	        }
   923	        visible = true
   924	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   925	        visible = false
   926	    }
   927	
   928	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   929	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   930	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   931	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   932	    )
   933	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   934	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   935	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   936	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   937	    )
   938	
   939	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   940	
   941	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   942	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   943	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   944	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   945	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   946	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   947	        } else {
   948	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   949	        }
   950	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   951	            stageScale = stageScale,
   952	            modifier = Modifier
   953	                .absoluteOffset(
   954	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   955	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   956	                )
   957	                .graphicsLayer {
   958	                    this.alpha = alpha
   959	                    scaleX = promptScale
   960	                    scaleY = promptScale
   961	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   962	                }
   963	        )
   964	    }
   965	}
   966	
   967	@Composable
   968	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   969	    stageScale: Float,
   970	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   971	) {
   972	    Box(
   973	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   974	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   975	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   976	        )
   977	    ) {
   978	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   979	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   980	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   981	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   982	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   983	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   984	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   985	                close()
   986	            }
   987	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   988	            drawRoundRect(
   989	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   990	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   991	                size = Size(
   992	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   993	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   994	                ),
   995	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   996	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   997	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   998	                )
   999	            )
  1000	            drawRoundRect(
  1001	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1002	                topLeft = Offset(
  1003	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
  1004	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
  1005	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
  1006	                ),
  1007	                size = Size(
  1008	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
  1009	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
  1010	                ),
  1011	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1012	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1013	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1014	                ),
  1015	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
  1016	            )
  1017	        }
  1018	        Text(
  1019	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
  1020	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1021	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1022	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
  1023	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1024	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
  1025	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1026	            modifier = Modifier
  1027	                .absoluteOffset(
  1028	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
  1029	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
  1030	                )
  1031	                .requiredSize(
  1032	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1033	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1034	                )
  1035	        )
  1036	    }
  1037	}
  1038	
  1039	@Composable
  1040	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
  1041	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1042	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
  1043	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
  1044	        p,
  1045	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
  1046	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
  1047	    )
  1048	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
  1049	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
  1050	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
  1051	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
  1052	    )
  1053	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
  1054	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1055	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1056	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1057	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1058	
  1059	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1060	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1061	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
  1062	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
  1063	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
  1064	
  1065	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
  1066	
  1067	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1068	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1069	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1070	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1071	                end = baseBounds,
  1072	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1073	            )
  1074	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1075	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1076	                end = baseBounds,
  1077	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1078	            )
  1079	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1080	
  1081	            drawFigmaImage(
  1082	                image = images.logoSimple,
  1083	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1084	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1085	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1086	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1087	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1088	            )
  1089	            drawFigmaImage(
  1090	                image = images.logoEyes,
  1091	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1092	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1093	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1094	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1095	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1096	            )
  1097	        }
  1098	
  1099	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1100	            val y = avatarYOffset
  1101	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1102	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1103	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1104	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1105	
  1106	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1107	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1108	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1109	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1110	        }
  1111	    }
  1112	}

  1274	private data class IntroImages(

  1306	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1307	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1308	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1309	    stageLeft: Float,
  1310	    stageTop: Float,
  1311	    stageScale: Float,
  1312	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1313	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1314	) {
  1315	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1316	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1317	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1318	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1319	
  1320	    if (clipCircle) {
  1321	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1322	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1323	        }
  1324	        clipPath(clip) {
  1325	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1326	        }
  1327	    } else {
  1328	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1329	    }
  1330	}
  1331	
  1332	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1333	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1334	    left: Float,
  1335	    top: Float,
  1336	    width: Float,
  1337	    height: Float,
  1338	    alpha: Float
  1339	) {
  1340	    drawImage(
  1341	        image = image,
  1342	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1343	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1344	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1345	    )
  1346	}

  1348	@Composable
  1349	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1350	    scale: Float,
  1351	    entryProgress: Float,
  1352	    finalProgress: Float,
  1353	    alpha: Float
  1354	) {
  1355	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1356	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1357	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1358	        end = baseBounds,
  1359	        amount = entryProgress
  1360	    )
  1361	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1362	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1363	        end = baseBounds,
  1364	        amount = entryProgress
  1365	    )
  1366	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1367	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1368	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1369	
  1370	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1371	        base = baseBounds,
  1372	        target = simpleBounds,
  1373	        scale = scale,
  1374	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1375	        alpha = alpha
  1376	    )

  1378	        base = baseBounds,
  1379	        target = eyesBounds,
  1380	        scale = scale,
  1381	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1382	        alpha = alpha
  1383	    )
  1384	}

  1442	@Composable
  1443	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1444	    text: String,
  1445	    x: Float,
  1446	    y: Float,
  1447	    textSize: Float,
  1448	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1449	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1450	    scale: Float
  1451	) {
  1452	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1453	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1454	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1455	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1456	    }
  1457	    Box(
  1458	        modifier = Modifier
  1459	            .absoluteOffset(
  1460	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1461	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1462	            )
  1463	            .requiredSize(
  1464	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1465	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1466	            ),
  1467	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1468	    ) {
  1469	        Text(
  1470	            text = text,
  1471	            color = Color.White,
  1472	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1473	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1474	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1475	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1476	            maxLines = 1,
  1477	            softWrap = false,
  1478	            textAlign = textAlign
  1479	        )
  1480	    }
  1481	}

  1543	@Composable
  1544	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1545	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1546	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1547	    scale: Float,
  1548	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1549	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1550	) {
  1551	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1552	    Image(
  1553	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1554	        contentDescription = null,
  1555	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1556	        modifier = Modifier
  1557	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1558	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1559	            .graphicsLayer {
  1560	                this.alpha = alpha
  1561	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1562	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1563	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1564	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1565	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1566	            }
  1567	    )
  1568	}
  1569	
  1570	@Composable
  1571	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1572	    x: Float,
  1573	    y: Float,
  1574	    w: Float,
  1575	    h: Float,
  1576	    scale: Float,
  1577	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1578	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1579	) {
  1580	    Image(
  1581	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1582	        contentDescription = null,
  1583	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1584	        modifier = Modifier
  1585	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1586	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1587	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1588	    )
  1589	}

  1591	@Composable
  1592	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1593	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1594	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1595	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1596	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1597	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1598	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1599	
  1600	        Box(
  1601	            modifier = Modifier
  1602	                .fillMaxSize()
  1603	                .background(background)
  1604	        ) {
  1605	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1606	                modifier = Modifier
  1607	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1608	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1609	            ) {
  1610	                content(scale)
  1611	            }
  1612	        }
  1613	    }
  1614	}
  1615	
  1616	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1617	
  1618	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1619	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1620	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1621	    } else {
  1622	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1623	    }
  1624	}
  1625	
  1626	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1627	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1628	}
  1629	
  1630	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1631	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1632	}

  1634	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1635	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1636	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1637	        colors = listOf(
  1638	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1639	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1640	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1641	        )
  1642	    )
  1643	}
  1644	
  1645	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1646	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1647	)
  1648	
  1649	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1650	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1651	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1652	}
  1653	
  1654	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1655	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1656	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1657	}
  1658	
  1659	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1660	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1661	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1662	}
  1663	
  1664	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1665	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1666	}
  1667	
  1668	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1669	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1670	}
  1671	
  1672	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1673	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1674	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1675	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1676	    return base + bounce
  1677	}
  1678	
  1679	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1680	
  1681	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1682	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1683	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1684	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1685	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1686	)
  1687	
  1688	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1689	    save = { snapshot ->
  1690	        listOf(
  1691	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1692	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1693	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1694	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1695	            snapshot.emailText,
  1696	            snapshot.monthText,
  1697	            snapshot.dayText,
  1698	            snapshot.yearText,
  1699	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1700	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1701	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1702	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1703	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1704	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1705	        )
  1706	    },
  1707	    restore = { values ->
  1708	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(

  1710	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1711	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1712	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1713	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1714	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1715	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1716	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1717	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1718	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1719	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1720	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1721	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,

  1723	        )

   488	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   489	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   490	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   491	        return true
   492	    }
   493	
   494	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   495	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   496	
   497	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   498	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   499	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   500	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   501	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   502	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   503	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   504	
   505	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   506	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   507	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   508	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   509	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   510	        pressedMockField = null
   511	        pressedRememberMe = false
   512	        pressedBackChevron = false
   513	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   514	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   515	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   516	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   517	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   518	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   519	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   520	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   521	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   522	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   523	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   524	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   525	        activeComposingText = ""
   526	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   527	        focusedMockField = null
   528	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   529	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   530	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   531	
   532	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   533	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   534	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   535	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   536	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   537	        } else {
   538	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   539	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   540	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   541	        }
   542	
   543	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   544	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   545	        mockCreateUsernameRevealProgress = if (usernameText.isEmpty()) 0f else 1f
   546	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   547	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   548	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   549	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   550	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   551	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   552	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   553	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   554	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   555	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   556	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   557	        ) {
   558	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   559	        } else {
   560	            null
   561	        }
   562	
   563	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   564	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   565	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   566	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   567	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   568	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   569	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   570	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   571	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   572	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   573	        }
   574	
   575	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   576	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   577	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   578	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   579	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   580	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   581	    }

   583	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   584	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   585	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   586	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   587	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   588	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   589	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   590	        pressedMockField = null
   591	        pressedRememberMe = false
   592	        pressedBackChevron = false
   593	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   594	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   595	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   596	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   597	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   598	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   599	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   600	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   601	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   602	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   603	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   604	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   605	    }
