   607	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   608	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   609	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   610	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   611	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   612	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   613	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   617	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   618	
   619	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   620	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   621	        }
   622	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   623	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   624	            progress,
   625	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   626	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   627	        )
   628	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   629	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   630	
   631	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   632	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   633	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   634	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   635	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   636	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   637	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   638	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   639	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   640	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   641	        updateMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
   642	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   643	
   644	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   645	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   646	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   647	        }
   648	
   649	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   650	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   651	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   652	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   653	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   654	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   655	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   656	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   657	            }
   658	        }
   659	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   660	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   661	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   662	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   663	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   664	                    canvas,
   665	                    outgoingFlow,
   666	                    stage.left,
   667	                    stage.top,
   668	                    stage.scale,
   669	                    1f - switchMotion,
   670	                    formFocusMotion,
   671	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   672	                )
   673	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   674	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   675	                        canvas,
   676	                        incomingFlow,
   677	                        stage.left,
   678	                        stage.top,
   679	                        stage.scale,
   680	                        switchMotion,
   681	                        formFocusMotion,
   682	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   683	                    )
   684	                }
   685	            } else {
   686	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   687	            }
   688	        }
   689	        drawAccountPrompt(
   690	            canvas = canvas,
   691	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   692	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   693	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   694	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   695	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   696	            } else {
   697	                0f
   698	            },
   699	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   700	        )
   701	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   702	            canvas,
   703	            stage,
   704	            mockMotion,
   705	            formFocusMotion
   706	        )
   707	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   708	            drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(
   709	                canvas,
   710	                stage,
   711	                blackToPurple,
   712	                purpleToSettle
   713	            )
   714	            drawBackChevron(
   715	                canvas,
   716	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   717	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   718	                stage.left,
   719	                if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
   720	                    stage.top
   721	                } else {
   722	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   723	                },
   724	                stage.scale,
   725	                mockMotion
   726	            )
   727	        }
   728	
   729	        if (
   730	            progress < 1f ||
   731	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   732	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   733	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   734	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   735	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   736	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   737	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   738	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   739	            isMockInputDimAnimating() ||
   740	            isMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimating()
   741	        ) {
   742	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   743	        }
   744	    }
   745	
   746	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   747	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   748	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   749	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   750	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   751	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   752	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   753	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   754	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   755	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   756	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   757	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   758	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   759	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   760	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   761	                        return true
   762	                    }
   763	                    mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = isMockAuthScreenAtBottom()
   764	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   765	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   766	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   767	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   768	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   769	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   770	                    if (
   771	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   772	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   773	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   774	                    ) {
   775	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   776	                        return true
   777	                    }
   778	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   779	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   780	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   781	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   782	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   783	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   784	                    } else {
   785	                        null
   786	                    }
   787	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   788	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   789	                    } else {
   790	                        null
   791	                    }
   792	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   793	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   794	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   795	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   796	                    ) {
   797	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   798	                    } else {
   799	                        null
   800	                    }
   801	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   802	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   803	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   804	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   805	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   806	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   807	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   808	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   809	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   810	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   811	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   812	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   813	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   814	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   815	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   816	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   817	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   818	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   819	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   820	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   821	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   822	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   823	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   824	                    ) {
   825	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   826	                    } else {
   827	                        null
   828	                    }
   829	                    pressedMockField = if (
   830	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   831	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   832	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   833	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   834	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   835	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   836	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   837	                    ) {
   838	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   839	                    } else {
   840	                        null
   841	                    }
   842	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   843	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   844	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   845	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   846	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   847	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   848	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   849	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   850	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   851	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   852	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   853	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   854	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   855	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   856	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   857	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   858	                    return true
   859	                }
   860	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   861	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   862	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   863	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   864	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   865	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   866	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   867	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   868	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   869	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   870	                ) {
   871	                    null
   872	                } else {
   873	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   874	                }
   875	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   876	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   877	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   878	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   879	            }
   880	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   881	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   882	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   883	                        return true
   884	                    }
   885	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   886	                        return true
   887	                    }
   888	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   889	                        return true
   890	                    }
   891	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   892	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   893	                        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   894	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   895	                    }
   896	                    return true
   897	                }
   898	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   899	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   900	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   901	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   902	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   903	                    }
   904	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   905	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   906	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   907	                    }
   908	                    return true
   909	                }
   910	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   911	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   912	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   913	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   914	            }
   915	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   916	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   917	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   918	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   919	                    pressedMockField = null
   920	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   921	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   922	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   923	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   924	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   925	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   926	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   927	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   928	                    return true
   929	                }
   930	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   931	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   932	                    pressedMockField = null
   933	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   934	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   935	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   936	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   937	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   938	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   939	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   940	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   941	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   942	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   943	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   944	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   945	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   946	                    return true
   947	                }
   948	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   949	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   950	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   951	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   952	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   953	                        return true
   954	                    }
   955	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   956	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   957	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   958	                        }
   959	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   960	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   961	                        }
   962	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   963	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   964	                        }
   965	                        else -> null
   966	                    }
   967	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   968	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   969	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   970	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   971	                        super.performClick()
   972	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   973	                        return true
   974	                    }
   975	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   976	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   977	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   978	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   979	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   980	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   981	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   982	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   983	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   984	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   985	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   986	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   987	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   988	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   989	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   990	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   991	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   992	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   993	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   994	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   995	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   996	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   997	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   998	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   999	                        pressedOption != null &&
  1000	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
  1001	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
  1002	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1003	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
  1004	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
  1005	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
  1006	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1007	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
  1008	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1009	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
  1010	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1011	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1012	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
  1013	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
  1014	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
  1015	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
  1016	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
  1017	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
  1018	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
  1019	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
  1020	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1021	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1022	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1023	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1024	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1025	                    pressedMockField = null
  1026	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1027	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1028	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1029	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1030	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1031	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1032	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1033	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1034	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1035	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1036	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1037	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1038	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1039	                    super.performClick()
  1040	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1041	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1042	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1043	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1044	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1045	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1046	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1047	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1048	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1049	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1050	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1051	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1052	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1053	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1054	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1055	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1056	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1057	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1058	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1059	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1060	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1061	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1062	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1063	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1064	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1065	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1066	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1067	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1068	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1069	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1070	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1071	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1072	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1073	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1074	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1075	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1076	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1077	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1078	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1079	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1080	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1081	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1082	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1083	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1084	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1085	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1086	                        }
  1087	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1088	                    } else {
  1089	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1090	                    }
  1091	                    return true
  1092	                }
  1093	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1094	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1095	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1096	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1097	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1098	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1099	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1100	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1101	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1102	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1103	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1104	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1105	                    swipeDirection != null
  1106	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1107	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1108	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
  1109	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1110	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1111	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1112	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1113	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1114	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1115	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1116	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1117	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1118	                    true
  1119	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1120	                    super.performClick()
  1121	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1122	                    true
  1123	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1124	                    super.performClick()
  1125	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1126	                    true
  1127	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1128	                    super.performClick()
  1129	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1130	                    true
  1131	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1132	                    performClick()
  1133	                    true
  1134	                } else {
  1135	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1136	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1137	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1138	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1139	                    false
  1140	                }
  1141	            }
  1142	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1143	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1144	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1145	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1146	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1147	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1148	                pressedMockField = null
  1149	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1150	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1151	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1152	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1153	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1154	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1155	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1156	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1157	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1158	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1159	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1160	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1161	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1162	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1163	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  1164	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1165	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1166	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1167	                false
  1168	            }
  1169	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1170	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1171	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1172	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1173	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1174	        }
  1175	    }

  1177	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1178	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1179	    }
  1180	
  1181	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1182	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1183	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1184	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1185	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1186	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1187	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1188	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1189	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1190	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1191	        }
  1192	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1193	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1194	        } else {
  1195	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1196	        }
  1197	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1198	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1199	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1200	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1201	    }

  1203	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1204	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1205	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1206	        }
  1207	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1208	    }

  1210	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1211	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1212	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1213	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1214	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1215	        }
  1216	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1217	    }

  1219	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1220	        super.performClick()
  1221	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1222	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1223	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1224	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1225	        return true
  1226	    }

  1228	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1229	        canvas: Canvas,
  1230	        stageLeft: Float,
  1231	        stageTop: Float,
  1232	        stageScale: Float,
  1233	        progress: Float,
  1234	        alpha: Float
  1235	    ) {
  1236	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1237	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1238	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1239	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1240	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1241	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1242	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1243	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1244	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1245	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1246	        )
  1247	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1248	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1249	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1250	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1251	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1252	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1253	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1254	        )
  1255	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1256	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1257	            focusShiftY
  1258	        )
  1259	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1260	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1261	            focusShiftY
  1262	        )
  1263	
  1264	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1265	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1266	            return
  1267	        }
  1268	
  1269	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1270	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1271	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1272	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1273	    }

  1275	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1276	        canvas: Canvas,
  1277	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1278	        yOffset: Float,
  1279	        alpha: Float
  1280	    ) {
  1281	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1282	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1283	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1284	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1285	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1286	
  1287	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1288	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1289	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1290	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1291	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1292	            )
  1293	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1294	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1295	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1296	            )
  1297	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1298	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1299	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1300	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1301	            )
  1302	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1303	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1304	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1305	            )
  1306	        } else {
  1307	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1308	        }
  1309	    }

  1311	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarStack(
  1312	        canvas: Canvas,
  1313	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1314	        yOffset: Float,
  1315	        alpha: Float
  1316	    ) {
  1317	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1318	            drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha)
  1319	            return
  1320	        }
  1321	
  1322	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  1323	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1324	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1325	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1326	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1327	
  1328	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1329	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1330	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1331	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1332	    }

  1335	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1336	        canvas: Canvas,
  1337	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1338	        yOffset: Float,
  1339	        alpha: Float,
  1340	        profileIndex: Int,
  1341	        offsetX: Float
  1342	    ) {
  1343	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1344	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1345	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1346	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1347	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1348	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1349	        }
  1350	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1351	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1352	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1353	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1354	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1355	        }
  1356	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1357	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1358	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1359	            canvas,
  1360	            avatarBitmap,
  1361	            Bounds(
  1362	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1363	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1364	                avatarSize,
  1365	                avatarSize
  1366	            ),
  1367	            stage.left,
  1368	            stage.top,
  1369	            stage.scale,
  1370	            alpha
  1371	        )
  1372	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1373	            canvas,
  1374	            nameBitmap,
  1375	            Bounds(
  1376	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1377	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1378	                nameWidth,
  1379	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1380	            ),
  1381	            stage.left,
  1382	            stage.top,
  1383	            stage.scale,
  1384	            alpha
  1385	        )
  1386	    }
  1387	
  1388	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1389	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1390	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1391	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1392	            return
  1393	        }
  1394	
  1395	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1396	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1397	            0f,
  1398	            0f,
  1399	            0f,
  1400	            height.toFloat(),
  1401	            intArrayOf(
  1402	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1403	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1404	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1405	            ),
  1406	            null,
  1407	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1408	        )
  1409	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1410	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1411	
  1412	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1413	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1414	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1415	        }
  1416	    }
  1417	
  1418	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1419	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1420	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1421	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1422	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1423	    }
  1424	
  1425	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1426	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1427	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1428	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1429	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1430	    }
  1431	
  1432	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1433	        canvas: Canvas,
  1434	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1435	        bounds: Bounds,
  1436	        stageLeft: Float,
  1437	        stageTop: Float,
  1438	        stageScale: Float,
  1439	        alpha: Float
  1440	    ) {
  1441	        tempRect.set(
  1442	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1443	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1444	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1445	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1446	        )
  1447	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1448	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1449	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1450	    }
  1451	
  1452	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1453	        canvas: Canvas,
  1454	        stageLeft: Float,
  1455	        stageTop: Float,
  1456	        stageScale: Float,
  1457	        yOffset: Float,
  1458	        alpha: Float
  1459	    ) {
  1460	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(activeMockFlow) ||
  1461	            usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(outgoingMockFlow)
  1462	        ) {
  1463	            return
  1464	        }
  1465	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1466	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1467	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1468	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1469	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1470	            portraitAmount
  1471	        ) * stageScale
  1472	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1473	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1474	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1475	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1476	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1477	
  1478	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1479	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1480	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1481	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1482	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1483	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1484	            standardCenterY,
  1485	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1486	            portraitAmount
  1487	        )
  1488	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1489	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1490	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1491	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1492	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1493	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1494	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1495	        if (
  1496	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1497	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1498	        ) {
  1499	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1500	            canvas.drawText(
  1501	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1502	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1503	                createBaselineY,
  1504	                accountPromptPaint
  1505	            )
  1506	        }
  1507	        if (
  1508	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1509	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1510	        ) {
  1511	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1512	            canvas.drawText(
  1513	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1514	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1515	                signInBaselineY,
  1516	                formTitlePaint
  1517	            )
  1518	        }
  1519	        if (
  1520	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1521	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1522	        ) {
  1523	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1524	            canvas.drawText(
  1525	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1526	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1527	                forgotBaselineY,
  1528	                accountPromptPaint
  1529	            )
  1530	        }
  1531	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1532	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1533	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1534	    }
  1535	
  1536	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1537	        canvas: Canvas,
  1538	        stageLeft: Float,
  1539	        stageTop: Float,
  1540	        stageScale: Float,
  1541	        progress: Float,
  1542	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1543	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1544	    ) {
  1545	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1546	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1547	        }
  1548	
  1549	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1550	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1551	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1552	            return
  1553	        }
  1554	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1555	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1556	            return
  1557	        }
  1558	
  1559	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1560	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1561	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1562	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1563	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1564	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1565	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1566	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1567	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1568	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1569	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1570	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1571	
  1572	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1573	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1574	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1575	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1576	
  1577	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1578	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1579	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1580	            canvas.save()
  1581	            canvas.clipRect(
  1582	                0f,
  1583	                stageTop + portraitLogoVisibleBottom() * stageScale,
  1584	                width.toFloat(),
  1585	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1586	            )
  1587	        }
  1588	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1589	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1590	            canvas.restore()
  1591	            canvas.save()
  1592	            canvas.clipRect(
  1593	                0f,
  1594	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1595	                width.toFloat(),
  1596	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1597	            )
  1598	        }
  1599	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1600	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1601	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1602	            canvas,
  1603	            fieldX,
  1604	            rememberMeY,
  1605	            contentLeft,
  1606	            contentTop,
  1607	            contentScale,
  1608	            alpha
  1609	        )
  1610	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1611	            canvas,
  1612	            contentLeft,
  1613	            contentTop,
  1614	            contentScale,
  1615	            alpha,
  1616	            yOffset,
  1617	            focusShiftY
  1618	        )
  1619	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1620	            canvas.restore()
  1621	        }
  1622	
  1623	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1624	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1625	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1626	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1627	    }

  1884	
  1885	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1886	        canvas: Canvas,
  1887	        stageLeft: Float,
  1888	        stageTop: Float,
  1889	        stageScale: Float,
  1890	        alpha: Float,
  1891	        yOffset: Float,
  1892	        focusShiftY: Float
  1893	    ) {
  1894	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1895	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1896	
  1897	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1898	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1899	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1900	
  1901	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1902	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1903	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1904	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1905	
  1906	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1907	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1908	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1909	                canvas,
  1910	                activeDropdown,
  1911	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1912	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1913	                stageLeft,
  1914	                stageTop,
  1915	                stageScale,
  1916	                alpha,
  1917	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1918	            )
  1919	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1920	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1921	            }
  1922	            return
  1923	        }
  1924	
  1925	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1926	        drawMockSignInField(
  1927	            canvas,
  1928	            activeField,
  1929	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1930	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1931	            stageLeft,
  1932	            stageTop,
  1933	            stageScale,
  1934	            alpha,
  1935	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1936	        )
  1937	    }

  1939	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1940	        canvas: Canvas,
  1941	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1942	        stageLeft: Float,
  1943	        stageTop: Float,
  1944	        stageScale: Float,
  1945	        progress: Float,
  1946	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1947	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1948	    ) {
  1949	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1950	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1951	        try {
  1952	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1953	                canvas,
  1954	                stageLeft,
  1955	                stageTop,
  1956	                stageScale,
  1957	                progress,
  1958	                formFocusMotion,
  1959	                updateLabelAnimation
  1960	            )
  1961	        } finally {
  1962	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1963	        }
  1964	    }

  1966	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1967	        canvas: Canvas,
  1968	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1969	        stageLeft: Float,
  1970	        stageTop: Float,
  1971	        stageScale: Float,
  1972	        alpha: Float
  1973	    ) {
  1974	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1975	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  1976	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  1977	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1978	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1979	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1980	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1981	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  1982	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  1983	        } else {
  1984	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  1985	        }) * stageScale
  1986	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1987	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1988	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1989	        val targetBaselineY = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  1990	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1991	            fieldTopY,
  1992	            stageTop,
  1993	            stageScale,
  1994	            targetTextSize
  1995	        )
  1996	
  1997	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1998	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1999	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2000	        canvas.drawText(
  2001	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  2002	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  2003	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  2004	            formTitlePaint
  2005	        )
  2006	    }

  2100	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2101	        title: String,
  2102	        contentTopY: Float,
  2103	        stageTop: Float,
  2104	        stageScale: Float,
  2105	        targetTextSize: Float
  2106	    ): Float {
  2107	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2108	        val titlePath = Path()
  2109	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2110	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2111	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2112	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2113	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2114	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2115	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2116	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2117	    }

  2119	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2120	        canvas: Canvas,
  2121	        x: Float,
  2122	        y: Float,
  2123	        stageLeft: Float,
  2124	        stageTop: Float,
  2125	        stageScale: Float,
  2126	        alpha: Float,
  2127	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2128	    ) {
  2129	        tempRect.set(
  2130	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2131	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2132	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2133	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2134	        )
  2135	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2136	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2137	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2138	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2139	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2140	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2141	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2142	            pressed -> 92f
  2143	            enabled -> 68f
  2144	            else -> 18f
  2145	        }
  2146	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2147	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2148	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2149	            pressed -> 238f
  2150	            enabled -> 210f
  2151	            else -> 118f
  2152	        }
  2153	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2154	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2155	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2156	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2157	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2158	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2159	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2160	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2161	        }
  2162	
  2163	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2164	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2165	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2166	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2167	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2168	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2169	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2170	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2171	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2172	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2173	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2174	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2175	            formTitlePaint
  2176	        )
  2177	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2178	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2179	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2180	            enabled -> 255f
  2181	            else -> 138f
  2182	        }).roundToInt()
  2183	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2184	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2185	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2186	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2187	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2188	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2189	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2190	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2191	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2192	            canvas.drawLine(
  2193	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2194	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2195	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2196	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2197	                backChevronPaint
  2198	            )
  2199	            canvas.drawLine(
  2200	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2201	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2202	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2203	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2204	                backChevronPaint
  2205	            )
  2206	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2207	        } else {
  2208	            tempRect.centerX()
  2209	        }
  2210	        canvas.drawText(
  2211	            buttonText,
  2212	            textX,
  2213	            baselineY,
  2214	            formTitlePaint
  2215	        )
  2216	    }
  2217	
  2218	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2219	        canvas: Canvas,
  2220	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2221	        radius: Float,
  2222	        alpha: Float
  2223	    ) {
  2224	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2225	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2226	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2227	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2228	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2229	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2230	            sweepLeft,
  2231	            buttonRect.top,
  2232	            sweepRight,
  2233	            buttonRect.top,
  2234	            intArrayOf(
  2235	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2236	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2237	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2238	            ),
  2239	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2240	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2241	        )
  2242	        canvas.save()
  2243	        tempPath.reset()
  2244	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2245	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2246	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2247	        canvas.restore()
  2248	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2249	    }

  2251	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  2252	        canvas: Canvas,
  2253	        x: Float,
  2254	        y: Float,
  2255	        stageLeft: Float,
  2256	        stageTop: Float,
  2257	        stageScale: Float,

  2260	    ) {
  2261	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale

  2277	        )

  2292	            rowTop,
  2293	            layout.boxWidth,
  2294	            stageScale,
  2295	            alpha
  2296	        )
  2297	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2298	            canvas,
  2299	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  2300	            layout.passwordBoxX,
  2301	            rowTop,
  2302	            layout.boxWidth,
  2303	            stageScale,
  2304	            alpha
  2305	        )
  2306	    }
  2307	
  2308	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2309	        canvas: Canvas,
  2310	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2311	        left: Float,
  2312	        top: Float,
  2313	        boxWidth: Float,
  2314	        stageScale: Float,
  2315	        alpha: Float
  2316	    ) {
  2317	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2318	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2319	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2320	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2321	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2322	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2323	            pressed -> 82f
  2324	            selected -> 64f
  2325	            else -> 14f
  2326	        }).roundToInt()
  2327	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2328	            pressed -> 245f
  2329	            selected -> 235f
  2330	            else -> 142f
  2331	        }).roundToInt()
  2332	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2333	
  2334	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2335	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2336	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2337	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2338	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2339	
  2340	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2341	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2342	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2343	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2344	        canvas.drawText(
  2345	            target.displayText,
  2346	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2347	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2348	            formInputPaint
  2349	        )
  2350	    }
