  2352	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2353	        canvas: Canvas,
  2354	        text: String,
  2355	        centerX: Float,
  2356	        topY: Float,
  2357	        maxWidth: Float,
  2358	        stageLeft: Float,
  2359	        stageTop: Float,
  2360	        stageScale: Float,
  2361	        alpha: Float
  2362	    ): Float {
  2363	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2364	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2365	            canvas,
  2366	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2367	            centerX,
  2368	            topY,
  2369	            maxWidth,
  2370	            stageLeft,
  2371	            stageTop,
  2372	            stageScale,
  2373	            alpha,
  2374	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2375	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2376	            forceLineBreaks = true
  2377	        )
  2378	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2379	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2380	            canvas,
  2381	            middleText,
  2382	            centerX,
  2383	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2384	            maxWidth,
  2385	            stageLeft,
  2386	            stageTop,
  2387	            stageScale,
  2388	            alpha,
  2389	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2390	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2391	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2392	        )
  2393	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2394	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2395	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2396	            canvas,
  2397	            bottomText,
  2398	            centerX,
  2399	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2400	            maxWidth,
  2401	            stageLeft,
  2402	            stageTop,
  2403	            stageScale,
  2404	            alpha,
  2405	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2406	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2407	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2408	        )
  2409	    }
  2410	
  2411	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2412	        canvas: Canvas,
  2413	        text: String,
  2414	        centerX: Float,
  2415	        topY: Float,
  2416	        maxWidth: Float,
  2417	        stageLeft: Float,
  2418	        stageTop: Float,
  2419	        stageScale: Float,
  2420	        alpha: Float,
  2421	        textSize: Float,
  2422	        lineHeight: Float,
  2423	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER,
  2424	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  2425	    ): Float {
  2426	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2427	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2428	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2429	
  2430	        val lines = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  2431	            text.split('\n').flatMap { line ->
  2432	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2433	            }
  2434	        } else {
  2435	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2436	        }
  2437	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2438	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2439	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2440	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2441	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2442	        }
  2443	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2444	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2445	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2446	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2447	        }
  2448	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2449	    }
  2450	
  2451	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2452	        canvas: Canvas,
  2453	        columnX: Float,
  2454	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2455	        stageLeft: Float,
  2456	        stageTop: Float,
  2457	        stageScale: Float,
  2458	        alpha: Float
  2459	    ) {
  2460	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2461	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, firstFieldY)
  2462	        val usesSeparatedMotionLayers = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
  2463	            (activeMockCreateAvatarDrag && mockCreateAvatarDragMoved)
  2464	        if (usesSeparatedMotionLayers) {
  2465	            val carouselAnimating = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2466	            val motionProgress = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2467	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2468	            } else {
  2469	                mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
  2470	            }
  2471	            val startBlend = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(
  2472	                (motionProgress / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2473	            )
  2474	            val endBlend = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2475	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(
  2476	                    ((motionProgress - (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS)) /
  2477	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2478	                )
  2479	            } else {
  2480	                0f
  2481	            }
  2482	            val effectiveMotionProgress = when {
  2483	                motionProgress <= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS -> 0f
  2484	                carouselAnimating &&
  2485	                    motionProgress >= 1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS -> 1f
  2486	                carouselAnimating -> {
  2487	                    (motionProgress - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS) /
  2488	                        (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS * 2f)
  2489	                }
  2490	                else -> {
  2491	                    (motionProgress - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS) /
  2492	                        (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS)
  2493	                }
  2494	            }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2495	            val separatedAlpha = alpha * minOf(startBlend, 1f - endBlend)
  2496	            canvas.save()
  2497	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2498	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2499	                canvas, createAvatarLayers.characters, avatarBounds,
  2500	                stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, separatedAlpha, effectiveMotionProgress
  2501	            )
  2502	            canvas.restore()
  2503	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2504	                canvas,
  2505	                createAvatarLayers.bubble,
  2506	                avatarBounds,
  2507	                stageLeft,
  2508	                stageTop,
  2509	                stageScale,
  2510	                separatedAlpha
  2511	            )
  2512	            canvas.save()
  2513	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2514	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2515	                canvas, createAvatarLayers.bottomStrokes, avatarBounds,
  2516	                stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, separatedAlpha, effectiveMotionProgress
  2517	            )
  2518	            canvas.restore()
  2519	            if (startBlend < 1f) {
  2520	                val sourceIndex = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2521	                    mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex
  2522	                } else {
  2523	                    mockCreateAvatarIndex
  2524	                }
  2525	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2526	                    canvas,
  2527	                    createAvatarBitmaps[sourceIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2528	                    avatarBounds,
  2529	                    stageLeft,
  2530	                    stageTop,
  2531	                    stageScale,
  2532	                    alpha * (1f - startBlend)
  2533	                )
  2534	            }
  2535	            if (endBlend > 0f) {
  2536	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2537	                    canvas,
  2538	                    createAvatarBitmaps[
  2539	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2540	                    ],
  2541	                    avatarBounds,
  2542	                    stageLeft,
  2543	                    stageTop,
  2544	                    stageScale,
  2545	                    alpha * endBlend
  2546	                )
  2547	            }
  2548	        } else {
  2549	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2550	                canvas,
  2551	                createAvatarBitmaps[mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2552	                avatarBounds,
  2553	                stageLeft,
  2554	                stageTop,
  2555	                stageScale,
  2556	                alpha
  2557	            )
  2558	        }
  2559	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2560	            canvas,
  2561	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2562	            avatarBounds,
  2563	            stageLeft,
  2564	            stageTop,
  2565	            stageScale,
  2566	            alpha
  2567	        )
  2568	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2569	            canvas,
  2570	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2571	            avatarBounds,
  2572	            stageLeft,
  2573	            stageTop,
  2574	            stageScale,
  2575	            alpha
  2576	        )
  2577	        val usernameBaselineY = stageTop + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(firstFieldY) * stageScale
  2578	        drawMockCreateUsername(canvas, centerX, usernameBaselineY, stageScale, alpha)
  2579	    }

  2581	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2582	        canvas: Canvas,
  2583	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2584	        mockMotion: Float,
  2585	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2586	    ) {
  2587	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2588	
  2589	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2590	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2591	                canvas,
  2592	                stage,
  2593	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2594	                formFocusMotion
  2595	            )
  2596	            return
  2597	        }
  2598	
  2599	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2600	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2601	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2602	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2603	            }
  2604	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2605	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2606	            }
  2607	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2608	            else -> return
  2609	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2610	
  2611	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2612	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2613	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2614	
  2615	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2616	            canvas.save()
  2617	            canvas.clipRect(
  2618	                0f,
  2619	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2620	                width.toFloat(),
  2621	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2622	            )
  2623	        }
  2624	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2625	            canvas,
  2626	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2627	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2628	            contentStage.left,
  2629	            contentStage.top,
  2630	            contentStage.scale,
  2631	            1f
  2632	        )
  2633	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2634	            canvas.restore()
  2635	        }
  2636	    }

  2638	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2639	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2640	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2641	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2642	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2643	    }

  2645	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(

  2647	        stage: StageMetrics,

  2650	    ) {

  2656	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)

  2669	            )
  2670	        }

  2674	            contentStage.top + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(sectionY) * contentStage.scale,

  2696	            settledAvatarBounds,

  2698	            settledContentStage.top,
  2699	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2700	            motion,
  2701	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2702	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2703	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2704	                settledContentStage,
  2705	                motion
  2706	            )
  2707	        )
  2708	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2709	            canvas.restore()
  2710	        }
  2711	
  2712	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2713	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2714	                stage.scale,
  2715	            (settledContentStage.top + settledAvatarBounds.y * settledContentStage.scale - stage.top) /
  2716	                stage.scale,
  2717	            settledAvatarBounds.w * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale,
  2718	            settledAvatarBounds.h * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale
  2719	        )
  2720	        val sourceBounds = mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds()
  2721	        val movingBounds = lerpBounds(sourceBounds, destinationBounds, motion)
  2722	        val avatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2723	
  2724	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  2725	            canvas,
  2726	            createAvatarBitmaps[avatarIndex],
  2727	            movingBounds,
  2728	            stage.left,
  2729	            stage.top,
  2730	            stage.scale,
  2731	            1f
  2732	        )
  2733	    }

  2735	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2736	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2737	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2738	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2739	        motion: Float
  2740	    ): Float {

  2745	            -halfChevronWidth

  2747	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2748	        }
  2749	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2750	    }

  2769	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(
  2770	        canvas: Canvas,
  2771	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2772	        motion: Float
  2773	    ) {
  2774	        val exitY = mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY() * motion
  2775	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2776	            val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2777	            val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  2778	                CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  2779	                CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  2780	                CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  2781	                CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  2782	            }
  2783	            val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) *
  2784	                PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  2785	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2786	                canvas,
  2787	                nameBitmap,
  2788	                Bounds(
  2789	                    (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  2790	                    portraitProfileNameTop() + exitY,
  2791	                    nameWidth,
  2792	                    portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2793	                ),
  2794	                stage.left,
  2795	                stage.top,
  2796	                stage.scale,
  2797	                1f
  2798	            )
  2799	            return
  2800	        }
  2801	
  2802	        val selectedIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2803	        val avatarBitmaps = listOf(steveAvatar, martinAvatar, jannyAvatar, guestAvatar)
  2804	        val nameBitmaps = listOf(steveName, martinName, jannyName, guestName)
  2805	        val nameWidths = listOf(220f, 220f, 220f, 200f)
  2806	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2807	        FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.forEachIndexed { index, target ->
  2808	            if (index != selectedIndex) {
  2809	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2810	                    canvas,
  2811	                    avatarBitmaps[index],
  2812	                    Bounds(
  2813	                        target.bounds.x,
  2814	                        target.bounds.y + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2815	                        target.bounds.w,
  2816	                        target.bounds.h
  2817	                    ),
  2818	                    stage.left,
  2819	                    stage.top,
  2820	                    stage.scale,
  2821	                    1f
  2822	                )
  2823	            }
  2824	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2825	                canvas,
  2826	                nameBitmaps[index],
  2827	                Bounds(
  2828	                    target.bounds.x - if (index == 3) 12f else 22f,
  2829	                    SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2830	                    nameWidths[index],
  2831	                    72f
  2832	                ),
  2833	                stage.left,
  2834	                stage.top,
  2835	                stage.scale,
  2836	                1f
  2837	            )
  2838	        }
  2839	    }

  2844	        } else {
  2845	            FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT - (SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + signInStackShiftY()) + 72f

  2849	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  2850	        canvas: Canvas,
  2851	        centerX: Float,
  2852	        baselineY: Float,
  2853	        stageScale: Float,
  2854	        alpha: Float
  2855	    ) {
  2856	        if (usernameText.isEmpty()) return
  2857	
  2858	        val maxTextWidth =
  2859	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) * stageScale
  2860	        val preferredTextSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2861	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  2862	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(usernameText)
  2863	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  2864	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  2865	        } else {
  2866	            preferredTextSize
  2867	        }
  2868	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2869	
  2870	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2871	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2872	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2873	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  2874	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  2875	            0f,
  2876	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  2877	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  2878	        )
  2879	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  2880	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  2881	
  2882	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2883	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2884	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  2885	
  2886	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  2887	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  2888	    }

  2911	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2912	        canvas: Canvas,
  2913	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  2914	        bounds: Bounds,
  2915	        stageLeft: Float,
  2916	        stageTop: Float,
  2917	        stageScale: Float,
  2918	        alpha: Float,
  2919	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  2920	    ) {
  2921	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2922	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2923	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  2924	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2925	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  2926	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2927	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2928	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2929	            dragDirection != null &&
  2930	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2931	
  2932	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  2933	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2934	                canvas,
  2935	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2936	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  2937	                stageLeft,
  2938	                stageTop,
  2939	                stageScale,
  2940	                alpha
  2941	            )
  2942	        }
  2943	
  2944	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  2945	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2946	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  2947	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  2948	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2949	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  2950	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  2951	        } else {
  2952	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  2953	        }
  2954	    }
  2955	
  2956	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  2957	        canvas: Canvas,
  2958	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2959	        stageLeft: Float,
  2960	        stageTop: Float,
  2961	        stageScale: Float
  2962	    ) {
  2963	        tempPath.reset()
  2964	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2965	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2966	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2967	            avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO * stageScale,
  2968	            Path.Direction.CW
  2969	        )
  2970	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2971	    }
  2972	
  2973	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2974	        canvas: Canvas,
  2975	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2976	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2977	        stageLeft: Float,
  2978	        stageTop: Float,
  2979	        stageScale: Float,
  2980	        alpha: Float,
  2981	        centerXOverridePx: Float? = null
  2982	    ) {
  2983	        val centerX = centerXOverridePx ?:
  2984	            stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2985	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2986	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2987	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2988	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2989	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2990	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2991	
  2992	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2993	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2994	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2995	        } else {
  2996	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2997	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2998	        }
  2999	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3000	    }
  3001	
  3002	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  3003	        canvas: Canvas,
  3004	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3005	        x: Float,
  3006	        y: Float,
  3007	        stageLeft: Float,
  3008	        stageTop: Float,
  3009	        stageScale: Float,
  3010	        alpha: Float,
  3011	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  3012	    ) {
  3013	        tempRect.set(
  3014	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  3015	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  3016	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  3017	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  3018	        )
  3019	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  3020	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3021	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3022	
  3023	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3024	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  3025	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3026	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3027	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  3028	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  3029	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  3030	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  3031	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  3032	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3033	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  3034	        } else {
  3035	            0f
  3036	        }
  3037	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  3038	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3039	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3040	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3041	                canvas = canvas,
  3042	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  3043	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  3044	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  3045	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  3046	                alpha = textAlpha
  3047	            )
  3048	        }
  3049	        canvas.drawText(
  3050	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  3051	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  3052	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  3053	            formInputPaint
  3054	        )
  3055	
  3056	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  3057	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  3058	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  3059	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3060	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  3061	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3062	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3063	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3064	    }

  3066	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  3067	        canvas: Canvas,
  3068	        stageLeft: Float,
  3069	        stageTop: Float,
  3070	        stageScale: Float,
  3071	        alpha: Float
  3072	    ) {
  3073	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  3074	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  3075	
  3076	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3077	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  3078	            dropdown,
  3079	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  3080	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3081	        )
  3082	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  3083	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  3084	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  3085	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  3086	        val optionAlpha = 255
  3087	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  3088	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3089	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  3090	        } else {
  3091	            0f
  3092	        }
  3093	
  3094	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  3095	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  3096	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  3097	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  3098	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  3099	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  3100	            } else {
  3101	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  3102	            }
  3103	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  3104	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  3105	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  3106	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  3107	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  3108	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3109	
  3110	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  3111	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  3112	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3113	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3114	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  3115	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  3116	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3117	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  3118	            } else {
  3119	                0f
  3120	            }
  3121	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3122	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3123	                    canvas = canvas,
  3124	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  3125	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  3126	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  3127	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  3128	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  3129	                )
  3130	            }
  3131	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  3132	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3133	            canvas.drawText(
  3134	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  3135	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  3136	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  3137	                formInputPaint
  3138	            )
  3139	        }
  3140	
  3141	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3142	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3143	                canvas,
  3144	                dropdown,
  3145	                top,
  3146	                right,
  3147	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  3148	                stageScale,
  3149	                optionAlpha
  3150	            )
  3151	        }
  3152	    }

  3154	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3155	        canvas: Canvas,
  3156	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3157	        left: Float,
  3158	        centerY: Float,
  3159	        iconSize: Float,
  3160	        alpha: Int
  3161	    ) {
  3162	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3163	        val width: Float
  3164	        val height: Float
  3165	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3166	            width = iconSize
  3167	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3168	        } else {
  3169	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3170	            height = iconSize
  3171	        }
  3172	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3173	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3174	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3175	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  3176	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  3177	    }

  3179	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3180	        canvas: Canvas,
  3181	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3182	        top: Float,
  3183	        right: Float,
  3184	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3185	        stageScale: Float,
  3186	        alpha: Int
  3187	    ) {
  3188	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3189	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3190	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3191	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3192	
  3193	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3194	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3195	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3196	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3197	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3198	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3199	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3200	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3201	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3202	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3203	
  3204	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  3205	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3206	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3207	
  3208	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3209	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3210	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3211	    }

  3213	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  3214	        canvas: Canvas,
  3215	        field: MockSignInField,
  3216	        x: Float,
  3217	        y: Float,
  3218	        stageLeft: Float,
  3219	        stageTop: Float,
  3220	        stageScale: Float,
  3221	        alpha: Float,
  3222	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  3223	    ) {
  3224	        tempRect.set(
  3225	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  3226	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  3227	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  3228	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  3229	        )
  3230	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  3231	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3232	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3233	
  3234	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  3235	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  3236	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3237	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  3238	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  3239	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  3240	            labelProgress
  3241	        ) * stageScale
  3242	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3243	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3244	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  3245	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3246	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  3247	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  3248	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3249	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3250	            labelProgress
  3251	        )
  3252	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  3253	        canvas.drawText(
  3254	            label,
  3255	            labelX,
  3256	            labelBaselineY,
  3257	            formLabelPaint
  3258	        )
  3259	
  3260	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  3261	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  3262	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  3263	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  3264	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  3265	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3266	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3267	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3268	
  3269	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  3270	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  3271	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  3272	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  3273	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  3274	
  3275	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  3276	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  3277	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3278	                canvas.drawLine(
  3279	                    caretX,
  3280	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  3281	                    caretX,
  3282	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  3283	                    formCaretPaint
  3284	                )
  3285	            }
  3286	        }
  3287	    }

  3289	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  3290	        canvas: Canvas,
  3291	        x: Float,
  3292	        y: Float,
  3293	        stageLeft: Float,
  3294	        stageTop: Float,
  3295	        stageScale: Float,
  3296	        alpha: Float
  3297	    ) {
  3298	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3299	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  3300	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  3301	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  3302	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  3303	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  3304	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  3305	
  3306	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)

  3310	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  3311	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  3312	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  3313

  3315	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3316	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  3317	            canvas.drawLine(
  3318	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  3319	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,

  3325	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  3326	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  3327	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  3328	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  3329	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  3330	            )
  3331	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  3332	        }
  3333	
  3334	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  3335	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3336	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3337	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  3338	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3339	        canvas.drawText(
  3340	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  3341	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  3342	            baselineY,

  3345	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255

  3348	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  3349	        canvas: Canvas,
  3350	        centerX: Float,
  3351	        topY: Float,
  3352	        stageLeft: Float,
  3353	        stageTop: Float,
  3354	        stageScale: Float,
  3355	        alpha: Float
  3356	    ) {
  3357	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  3358	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3359	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  3360	        } else {
  3361	            1f
  3362	        }
  3363	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  3364	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  3365	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stageScale
  3366	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * chevronScale * stageScale / 2f
  3367	
  3368	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  3369	        val strokeScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3370	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_STROKE_SCALE
  3371	        } else {
  3372	            1f
  3373	        }
  3374	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth =
  3375	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * chevronScale * strokeScale * stageScale
  3376	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  3377	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  3378	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3379	    }

  3381	    private fun drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(

  3400	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  3401	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  3402	    }
  3403	
  3404	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3405	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3406	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3407	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3408	    )
  3409	
  3410	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3411	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3412	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3413	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3414	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3415	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3416	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3417	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3418	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3419	        )
  3420	    }
  3421	
  3422	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3423	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3424	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3425	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3426	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3427	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3428	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3429	
  3430	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3431	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3432	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3433	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3434	            }
  3435	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3436	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3437	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3438	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3439	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3440	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3441	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3442	                    sourceAlpha,
  3443	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3444	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3445	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3446	                )
  3447	            }
  3448	        }
  3449	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3450	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3451	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3452	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3453	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3454	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3455	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3456	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3457	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3458	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3459	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3460	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3461	                        sourceAlpha,
  3462	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3463	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3464	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3465	                    )
  3466	                }
  3467	            }
  3468	        }
  3469	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3470	    }
  3471	
  3472	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3473	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3474	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3475	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3476	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3477	    }
  3478	
  3479	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3480	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3481	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3482	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3483	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3484	
  3485	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3486	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3487	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3488	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3489	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3490	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3491	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3492	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3493	            val dx = x - centerX
  3494	            val dy = y - centerY
  3495	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3496	        }
  3497	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3498	    }
  3499	
  3500	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3501	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3502	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3503	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3504	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3505	
  3506	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3507	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3508	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3509	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3510	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3511	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3512	        var queueStart = 0
  3513	        var queueEnd = 0
  3514	
  3515	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3516	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3517	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3518	            val dx = x - centerX
  3519	            val dy = y - centerY
  3520	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3521	                distance[index] = 0
  3522	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3523	            }
  3524	        }
  3525	
  3526	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3527	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3528	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3529	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3530	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3531	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3532	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3533	                val nextY = y + dy
  3534	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3535	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3536	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3537	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3538	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3539	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3540	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3541	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3542	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3543	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3544	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3545	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3546	                }
  3547	            }
  3548	        }
  3549	
  3550	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3551	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3552	        }
  3553	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3554	    }
  3555	
  3556	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3557	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3558	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3559	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3560	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3561	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3562	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3563	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3564	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3565	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3566	    }
  3567	
  3568	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3569	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3570	    }
  3571	
  3572	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  3573	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  3574	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  3575	        }
  3576	    }
  3577	
  3578	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  3579	        return when {
  3580	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  3581	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  3582	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  3583	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  3584	            else -> null
  3585	        }
  3586	    }
  3587	
  3588	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  3589	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  3590	    }

  3592	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  3593	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  3594	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  3595	    }

  3597	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  3598	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  3599	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  3600	
  3601	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3602	
  3603	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3604	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  3605	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3606	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3607	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  3608	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  3609	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  3610	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  3611	                ) * stage.scale
  3612	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  3613	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  3614	            } else {
  3615	                null
  3616	            }
  3617	        }
  3618	
  3619	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  3620	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  3621	            val bounds = target.bounds
  3622	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  3623	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  3624	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  3625	            val dx = x - centerX
  3626	            val dy = y - centerY
  3627	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  3628	        }?.profile
  3629	    }

  3858	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3859	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3860	
  3861	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3862	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3863	        if (
  3864	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3865	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3866	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3867	        ) {
  3868	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3869	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3870	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3871	            } else {
  3872	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3873	            }
  3874	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3875	        }
  3876	
  3877	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3878	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3879	        }
  3880	        return true
  3881	    }

  3883	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  3884	        if (
  3885	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3886	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3887	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3888	        ) {
  3889	            return null
  3890	        }
  3891	
  3892	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3893	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3894	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3895	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3896	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3897	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3898	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3899	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3900	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3901	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3902	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3903	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  3904	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3905	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  3906	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  3907	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  3908	        }
  3909	
  3910	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3911	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3912	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3913	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  3914	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  3915	        }
  3916	        return null
  3917	    }

  3919	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {

  3923	            expandedMockDropdown == null

  3927	
  3928	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3929	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3930	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3931	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3932	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3933	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3934	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3935	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3936	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3937	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3938	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3939	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  3940	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  3941	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  3942	    }
  3943	
  3944	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3945	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3946	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3947	
  3948	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3949	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3950	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3951	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3952	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3953	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3954	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3955	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3956	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3957	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3958	        }
  3959	
  3960	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3961	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3962	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3963	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3964	        }
  3965	        return true
  3966	    }
  3967	
  3968	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3969	        if (
  3970	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3971	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  3972	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3973	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3974	            ) ||
  3975	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  3976	        ) {
  3977	            return false
  3978	        }
  3979	
  3980	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3981	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  3982	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  3983	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  3984	            if (mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown && deltaY < 0f) return false
  3985	            mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  3986	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  3987	            pressedMockField = null
  3988	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3989	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3990	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3991	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  3992	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  3993	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  3994	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3995	        }
  3996	
  3997	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3998	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3999	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4000	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4001	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4002	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4003	            else -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4004	        }
  4005	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  4006	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4007	        return true
  4008	    }

  4013	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false

  4024	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4025	        if (
  4026	            mockDragReturnInProgress ||
  4027	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4028	            !mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown
  4029	        ) {
  4030	            return false
  4031	        }
  4032	
  4033	        val downwardScrollY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  4034	        if (downwardScrollY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  4035	
  4036	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4037	        return downwardScrollY > abs(dragX)
  4038	    }

  4040	    private fun isMockAuthScreenAtBottom(): Boolean {
  4041	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  4042	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return true
  4043	
  4044	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  4045	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4046	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4047	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(
  4048	            FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
  4049	        )
  4050	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4051	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4052	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4053	                stage,
  4054	                yOffset,
  4055	                focusShiftY
  4056	            )
  4057	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> 0f
  4058	            null -> return false
  4059	        }
  4060	        return portraitAuthScrollY >= maxScrollY - maxOf(1f, stage.scale)
  4061	    }

  4065	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  4066	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  4067	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  4068	    }

  4405	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4406	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4407	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4408	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4409	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4410	            null -> null
  4411	        }
  4412	        if (title != null) {
  4413	            val titleTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  4414	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  4415	                title,
  4416	                mockPrimaryFormY(),
  4417	                stage.top,
  4418	                stage.scale,
  4419	                titleTextSize
  4420	            )
  4421	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  4422	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  4423	                mockFormFieldGap() * stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4424	        }
  4425	        return stage.top +
  4426	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  4427	    }
  4428	
  4429	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4430	        val stickyBottomHeight =
  4431	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE *
  4432	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE * stage.scale
  4433	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  4434	            stickyBottomHeight * portraitStickyBottomMotion()
  4435	    }
  4436	
  4437	    private fun portraitAuthPointerInViewport(y: Float, stage: StageMetrics): Boolean {
  4438	        return !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  4439	            y in portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage)..portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  4440	    }
  4441	
  4442	    private fun mockCreateContentStageMetrics(
  4443	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4444	        yOffset: Float,
  4445	        focusShiftY: Float
  4446	    ): StageMetrics {
  4447	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return stage
  4448	
  4449	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4450	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4451	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4452	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  4453	        return StageMetrics(
  4454	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4455	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4456	            scale = contentScale
  4457	        )
  4458	    }
  4459	
  4460	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  4461	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4462	        yOffset: Float,
  4463	        focusShiftY: Float
  4464	    ): Float {
  4465	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4466	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4467	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4468	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  4469	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  4470	            avatarSectionY
  4471	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  4472	        val finalControlBottomY = mockDropdownY(
  4473	            MockDropdown.Year,
  4474	            yOffset,
  4475	            focusShiftY
  4476	        ) + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT
  4477	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY)
  4478	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4479	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4480	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4481	    }
  4482	
  4483	    private fun portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4484	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4485	        yOffset: Float,
  4486	        focusShiftY: Float
  4487	    ): Float {
  4488	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4489	        val contentStage = mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4490	        val helpBottomY = mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4491	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + helpBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4492	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4493	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4494	    }

  4569	
  4570	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  4571	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  4572	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  4573	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4574	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  4575	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  4576	            copyWidth,
  4577	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  4578	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4579	            forceLineBreaks = true
  4580	        )
  4581	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  4582	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4583	                middleText,
  4584	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4585	                copyWidth,
  4586	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4587	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4588	            )
  4589	        }
  4590	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  4591	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  4592	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4593	                bottomText,
  4594	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4595	                copyWidth,
  4596	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4597	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4598	            )
  4599	        }
  4600	        return bottomY
  4601	    }

  4606	        } else {
  4607	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_LANDSCAPE_BODY_TOP_GAP
  4608	        }
  4609	    }

  4611	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4612	        val helpTopY = mockForgotPasswordButtonY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  4613	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TOP_GAP
  4614	        return mockParagraphBottomY(
  4615	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  4616	            helpTopY,
  4617	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  4618	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  4619	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  4620	        )
  4621	    }
  4622	
  4623	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  4624	        text: String,
  4625	        topY: Float,
  4626	        maxWidth: Float,
  4627	        textSize: Float,
  4628	        lineHeight: Float,
  4629	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  4630	    ): Float {
  4631	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  4632	        val lineCount = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  4633	            text.split('\n').sumOf { line ->
  4634	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4635	            }
  4636	        } else {
  4637	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4638	        }
  4639	        return topY + lineCount * lineHeight
  4640	    }

  4642	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4643	        left: Float,
  4644	        width: Float,
  4645	        labelWidth: Float,
  4646	        boxWidth: Float
  4647	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  4648	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4649	        val labelX = left + gap
  4650	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  4651	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  4652	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4653	            labelX = labelX,
  4654	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  4655	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  4656	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  4657	        )
  4658	    }
  4659	
  4660	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  4661	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  4662	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  4663	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  4664	    ): Float {
  4665	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  4666	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4667	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  4668	    }

  4678	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  4679	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  4680	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  4681	
  4682	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  4683	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4684	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  4685	        } else {
  4686	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  4687	        }
  4688	        return when (field) {
  4689	            MockSignInField.Username,
  4690	            MockSignInField.Password,
  4691	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX
  4692	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  4693	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  4694	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  4695	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  4696	        }
  4697	    }

  4699	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4700	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4701	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4702	        }
  4703	
  4704	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  4705	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  4706	            field in listOf(
  4707	                MockSignInField.Email,
  4708	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4709	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4710	                MockSignInField.Year
  4711	            )
  4712	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  4713	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4714	        } else {
  4715	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4716	        }
  4717	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  4718	            when (field) {
  4719	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4720	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  4721	                } else {
  4722	                    0f
  4723	                }
  4724	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0f
  4725	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4726	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  4727	                } else {
  4728	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap() * 2f
  4729	                }
  4730	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4731	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  4732	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  4733	                } else {
  4734	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * 2f + mockFormFieldGap() * 3f
  4735	                }
  4736	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4737	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4738	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  4739	            }
  4740	        } else {
  4741	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  4742	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  4743	        }
  4744	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  4745	    }

  4766	    private fun mockBackChevronRowTopY(): Float {
  4767	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
  4768	            val stickyBottomHeight =
  4769	                MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  4770	            val chevronHeight =
  4771	                MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  4772	            val settledTop = portraitSafeBottom() - stickyBottomHeight +
  4773	                (stickyBottomHeight - chevronHeight) / 2f
  4774	            return settledTop + stickyBottomHeight * (1f - portraitStickyBottomMotion())
  4775	        }
  4776	        return ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY() +
  4777	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_ROW_TOP_GAP
  4778	    }
