  4897	    private fun mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4898	        return dropdown != MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier
  4899	    }
  4900	
  4901	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4902	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  4903	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  4904	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  4905	        } else {
  4906	            optionCount
  4907	        }
  4908	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  4909	    }
  4910	
  4911	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4912	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  4913	    }
  4914	
  4915	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4916	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4917	    }
  4918	
  4919	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4920	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4921	    }
  4922	
  4923	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  4924	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4925	    }
  4926	
  4927	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  4928	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  4929	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  4930	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  4931	    }
  4932	
  4933	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  4934	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4935	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  4936	            return
  4937	        }
  4938	
  4939	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  4940	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  4941	    }
  4942

  4946	            else -> mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)
  4947	        }
  4948	    }

  5078	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {

  5212	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  5213	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  5214	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  5215	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  5216	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  5217	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5218	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  5219	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  5220	    }

  5223	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  5224	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5225	    }

  5269	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,

  5368	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  5369	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  5370	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5371	        return if (
  5372	            focusedMockField != null ||
  5373	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  5374	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  5375	        ) {
  5376	            1f
  5377	        } else {
  5378	            0f
  5379	        }
  5380	    }
  5381	
  5382	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  5383	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  5384	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  5385	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  5386	    }
  5387	
  5388	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  5389	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  5390	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  5391	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  5392	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  5393	        }
  5394	
  5395	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  5396	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  5397	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  5398	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  5399	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  5400	    }

  5492	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  5493	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  5494	        val usernameWasEmpty = usernameText.isEmpty()
  5495	        when (field) {
  5496	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  5497	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  5498	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  5499	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  5500	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  5501	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  5502	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  5503	        }
  5504	        if (field == MockSignInField.Username && usernameWasEmpty != usernameText.isEmpty()) {
  5505	            restartMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
  5506	        }
  5507	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  5508	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5509	        }
  5510	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5511	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  5512	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  5513	        }
  5514	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5515	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5516	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()

  5520	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5521	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5522	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5523	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  5524	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5525	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5526	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5527	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  5528	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  5529	        activeComposingText = ""
  5530	        return true
  5531	    }
  5532	
  5533	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5534	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5535	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5536	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5537	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5538	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5539	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  5540	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  5541	        activeComposingText = composingText
  5542	        return true
  5543	    }
  5544	
  5545	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  5546	        activeComposingText = ""
  5547	        return true
  5548	    }
  5549	
  5550	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  5551	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5552	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5553	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  5554	            activeComposingText = ""
  5555	            return true
  5556	        }
  5557	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  5558	        activeComposingText = ""
  5559	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  5560	        return true
  5561	    }
  5562	
  5563	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5564	        activeComposingText = ""
  5565	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  5566	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  5567	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  5568	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  5569	        } else if (
  5570	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  5571	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  5572	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  5573	        ) {
  5574	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5575	        } else {
  5576	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  5577	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  5578	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5579	        }
  5580	        return true
  5581	    }
  5582	
  5583	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5584	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5585	        return when (keyCode) {
  5586	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5587	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5588	            else -> {
  5589	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5590	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  5591	            }
  5592	        }
  5593	    }
  5594	
  5595	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  5596	        return buildString {
  5597	            input.forEach { character ->
  5598	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  5599	                    append(character)
  5600	                }
  5601	            }
  5602	        }
  5603	    }
  5604	
  5605	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  5606	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  5607	
  5608	        var startIndex = 0
  5609	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  5610	            startIndex += 1
  5611	        }
  5612	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  5613	    }
  5614	
  5615	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  5616	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  5617	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  5618	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  5619	                lines += ""
  5620	            }
  5621	
  5622	            var currentLine = ""
  5623	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  5624	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  5625	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  5626	                .forEach { word ->
  5627	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  5628	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  5629	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  5630	                    } else {
  5631	                        lines += currentLine
  5632	                        currentLine = word
  5633	                    }
  5634	                }
  5635	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  5636	                lines += currentLine
  5637	            }
  5638	        }
  5639	        return lines
  5640	    }
  5641	
  5642	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  5643	        return when {
  5644	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  5645	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  5646	            else -> current
  5647	        }
  5648	    }

  5650	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  5651	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  5652	
  5653	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  5654	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  5655	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  5656	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  5657	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  5658	        return StageMetrics(
  5659	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  5660	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  5661	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  5662	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  5663	            scale = stageScale
  5664	        )
  5665	    }

  5680	
  5681	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  5682	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  5683	    }

  5884	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  5885	        if (
  5886	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  5887	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  5888	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  5889	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  5890	        ) {
  5891	            return
  5892	        }
  5893	
  5894	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  5895	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()

  5917	            emailText = ""

  5919	        }

  5922	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5923	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5924	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5925	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()

  5929	    }

  6020	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  6021	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  6022	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  6023	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6024	        return lerpFloat(
  6025	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  6026	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  6027	            progress
  6028	        )
  6029	    }
  6030	
  6031	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  6032	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  6033	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  6034	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6035	    }
  6036	
  6037	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  6038	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  6039	    }
  6040

  6042	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  6043	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  6044	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  6045	    }

  6047	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  6048	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  6049	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  6050	    }
  6051	
  6052	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  6053	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  6054	    }
  6055	
  6056	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  6057	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  6058	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  6059	            .roundToInt()
  6060	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  6061	            .toLong()
  6062	    }
  6063	
  6064	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  6065	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  6066	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  6067	
  6068	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  6069	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  6070	            activeMockFlow = null
  6071	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  6072	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  6073	            pressedMockField = null
  6074	            pressedRememberMe = false
  6075	            pressedBackChevron = false
  6076	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6077	            pressedMockDropdown = null

  6080	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6081	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null

  6083	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  6084	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6085	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  6086	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6087	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6088	            return
  6089	        }
  6090	
  6091	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  6092	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  6093	    }

  6095	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  6096	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  6097	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  6098	        }
  6099	
  6100	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {

  6106	        }

  6110	        }
  6111

  6113	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  6114	        }
  6115	
  6116	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  6117	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()

  6119	        }
  6120

  6122	            return ""
  6123	        }

  6126	            return ""
  6127	        }
  6128	
  6129	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  6130	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  6131	        }
  6132	
  6133	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  6134	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  6135	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  6136	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  6137	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  6138	                else -> {
  6139	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  6140	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  6141	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  6142	                    } else {
  6143	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  6144	                    }
  6145	                }
  6146	            }
  6147	        }
  6148	    }
  6149	}

  6305	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  6306	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  6307	}
  6308	
  6309	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  6310	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  6311	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6312	}
  6313	
  6314	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6315	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  6316	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6317	}
  6318	
  6319	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6320	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  6321	}
  6322	
  6323	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6324	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  6325	}
  6326	
  6327	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  6328	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6329	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  6330	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  6331	    return base + bounce
  6332	}
  6333	
  6334	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  6335	
  6336	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  6337	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  6338	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  6339	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  6340	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  6341	)
  6342	
  6343	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  6344	    x = bounds.x,
  6345	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  6346	    w = bounds.w,
  6347	    h = bounds.h
  6348	)
  6349	
  6350	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  6351	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6352	    return Color.argb(
  6353	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  6354	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  6355	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  6356	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  6357	    )
  6358	}
  6359	
  6360	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  6361	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  6362	}
  6363	
  6364	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  6365	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  6366	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  6367	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  6368	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  6369	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  6370	    return output
  6371	}
  6372	
  6373	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  6374	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  6375	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  6376	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  6377	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  6378	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  6379	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  6380	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  6381	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  6382	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  6383	    return output
  6384	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_SKIN="cinerific_pixel_tablet_1280"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_skin="$(sed -n 's/^skin.name=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    38	[[ "$config_skin" == "$EXPECTED_SKIN" ]] || fail "Expected skin $EXPECTED_SKIN, got $config_skin"
    39	[[ "$config_frame" == "yes" ]] || fail "Device frame is not enabled in $AVD_CONFIG"
    40	
    41	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    42	
    43	device_is_connected() {
    44	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    45	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    46	}
    47	
    48	if device_is_connected; then
    49	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    50	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    51	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    52	else
    53	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    54	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    55	  : > "$LOG"
    56	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    57	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    58	fi
    59	
    60	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    61	until device_is_connected; do
    62	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    63	  sleep 2
    64	done
    65	
    66	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    67	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    68	  sleep 2
    69	done
    70	
    71	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    72	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    74	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    75	
    76	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    77	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    78	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    79	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    80	
    81	sleep 10
    82	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    83	
    84	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    85	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    86	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    87	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	else
    89	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    90	fi
    91	
    92	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    93	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    94	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/launchd/com.cinerific.standardavd.plist" \
   177	    "$PROJECT_DIR/start_cinerific_android_standard.sh" \
   178	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   179	  do
   180	    print_file "$file"
   181	  done
   182	
   183	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   184	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — EMULATOR SKINS" "$PROJECT_DIR/emulator/skins"
   185	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   186	
   187	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   188	
   189	
   190	============================================================
   191	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   192	============================================================
   193	
   194	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   195	- .git
   196	- .gradle
   197	- .idea
   198	- app/build
   199	- build
   200	- .kotlin
   201	- .tmp
   202	- .cache
   203	- .DS_Store
   204	- local.properties
   205	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   206	- *.jks
   207	- *.keystore
   208	
   209	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   210	
   211	EOF
   212	}
   213	
   214	write_snapshot
   215	
   216	echo "Done."
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   219	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   220	echo ""
   221	echo "Open it with:"
   222	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
